*Maria Luongo,Davide Marocco,Nicola Milano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语义投影的无监督框架，用于直接从自然语言中测量抑郁、焦虑和担忧等心理状态，具有高可解释性和跨情境泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理特质语言评估方法多依赖有监督模型预测问卷得分，导致可解释性差、泛化能力弱。

Method: 将心理构念建模为基于临床量表词项定义的可解释语义轴；使用Sentence-BERT嵌入文本，并将其投影到这些轴上以生成连续心理得分；评估方式包括与临床量表的相关性、重测信度、Wasserstein距离分布相似性及与VADER对比。

Result: 投影得分与临床量表呈强相关（尤其在选词、写词、短语等结构化格式中）；自由文本整段分析效果较弱，但句子级聚合显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 语义投影是一种可解释、可扩展的无监督替代方案，响应格式与文本处理策略对语言心理评估至关重要。

Abstract: Recent advances in natural language processing have enabled increasingly accurate estimation of psychological traits from language. However, most existing approaches rely on supervised models trained to predict questionnaire scores, limiting interpretability and generalizability across contexts. The present study introduces a theory-driven and fully unsupervised framework for measuring psychological states directly from natural language using semantic projection. Psychological constructs were operationalized as interpretable semantic axes derived from lexical anchors and items from validated clinical scales assessing depression, anxiety, and worry. Participants textual responses were embedded using Sentence-BERT and projected onto these axes to generate continuous psychological scores across multiple response formats, including selected words, generated words, phrases, and free-text responses. Projection scores were evaluated through correlations with standardized clinical measures , split-half reliability analyses, attenuation corrections, distributional similarity using Wasserstein distance, and comparisons with lexicon-based sentiment analysis (VADER). Results showed strong associations between projection scores and clinical measures, particularly for structured formats such as selected words, written words, and phrases. Free-text responses produced weaker results when analyzed as whole texts, but performance improved substantially when sentence-level aggregation strategies were applied. These findings support semantic projection as an interpretable and scalable alternative to supervised language models for psychological assessment and highlight the importance of response format and text-processing strategies in language-based mental health measurement.

</details>


### [38] [Anticipating Innovation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04875)
*Enrico Maria Fenoaltea,Filippo Santoro,Giordano De Marzo,Segun Taofeek Aroyehun,Andrea Tacchella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TechToken模型，通过将国际专利分类代码视为词汇，利用Transformer架构学习技术语言，发现技术组合的早期预测信号源于集体语言变化而非个体发明者，并通过上下文相似性准确预测首次技术组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测技术创新（即新技术组合的出现）是科学和政策制定中的关键挑战，现有方法难以捕捉长期预测信号。

Method: 提出TechToken——一种基于Transformer的模型，将国际专利分类（IPC）代码作为词汇进行嵌入学习；定义代码嵌入间的上下文相似性以衡量技术语言收敛，并用于预测首次技术组合。

Result: 发现技术组合的预测信号可提前数十年在专利文本语言中被检测到，且该信号是集体性的；TechToken在多项专利相关任务上超越现有最优模型。

Conclusion: 技术语言的集体演化蕴含强预测能力，TechToken为技术创新预测提供了新范式，兼具理论意义与政策应用价值。

Abstract: Forecasting innovation, intended as the emergence of new technological combinations, is a fundamental challenge for science and policy. We show that forthcoming combinations leave an early trace in the collective language of patents, with predictive signals detectable even decades in advance. We show that signal is not attributable to any single inventor, but emerges as a collective shift in how technologies are described across thousands of patents. To this end, we introduce TechToken, a transformer-based model that treats technologies, classified by International Patent Classification codes, as words in its vocabulary, learning the language of technologies by embedding these codes during fine-tuning. We define context similarity between code embeddings as a measure of linguistic convergence and show that it accurately predicts first technological combinations. TechToken also improves general representation quality, outperforming state-of-the-art models across different patent-related tasks.

</details>

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了PyCaret AutoML和CNN-BiLSTM两种方法在印尼语Twitter仇恨言论检测任务上的性能，结果表明CNN-BiLSTM在准确率和F1分数上均优于PyCaret中的最佳模型（随机森林）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为评估不同建模范式（传统机器学习 vs. 深度学习）在印尼语短文本仇恨言论检测任务上的有效性，并在统一预处理下公平比较模型能力

Method: 构建两个分支：PyCaret AutoML分支（含TF-IDF+词典 abusive-word 计数）和CNN-BiLSTM分支（端到端学习token表示，捕获局部短语模式与双向上下文）；使用Ibrohim和Budi语料库的HS标签（13,130条，58:42类别比）进行训练与验证

Result: CNN-BiLSTM取得最优性能：准确率83.8%，精确率79.8%，召回率82.7%，F1分数81.2%；PyCaret中最佳模型Random Forest为77.2%准确率、77.0% F1；神经分支分别提升准确率6.6点、F1 4.2点

Conclusion: PyCaret AutoML是有效的传统基线框架，而CNN-BiLSTM在此印尼语仇恨言论检测任务中表现更优，尤其适用于依赖局部词汇线索与短上下文组合的短文本场景

Abstract: This paper compares a PyCaret AutoML branch and a CNN-BiLSTM branch for binary hate speech detection on Indonesian Twitter using the HS label from the corpus of Ibrohim and Budi. Both branches share the same preprocessing pipeline so that the comparison reflects modelling differences rather than inconsistent data preparation. The conventional branch uses TF-IDF with a lexicon-based abusive-word count, whereas the neural branch learns dense token representations and captures both local phrase patterns and bidirectional context. The benchmark is built from the released 13,130-row annotation table, whose HS label yields a 58:42 class ratio. On the held-out split, CNN-BiLSTM achieves the best result with 83.8% accuracy, 79.8% precision, 82.7% recall, and 81.2% F1-score. Within the PyCaret branch, Random Forest is the strongest conventional model with 77.2% accuracy and 77.0% F1-score. The neural branch therefore improves accuracy by 6.6 points and F1-score by 4.2 points. Exploratory corpus analysis, learning curves, and confusion matrices show that the dataset is short-text, moderately imbalanced, and still difficult because many decisions depend on local lexical cues plus short contextual composition. The study concludes that PyCaret AutoML is an effective conventional benchmarking framework, whereas CNN-BiLSTM is the stronger end model for the reported benchmark setting.

</details>


### [40] [BenCSSmark: Making the Social Sciences Count in LLM Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04886)
*Arnault Chatelain,Étienne Ollion,Qianwen Guan,Diandra Fabre,Lorraine Goeuriot,Emile Chapuis,Abdelkrim Beloued,Marie Candito,Nicolas Hervé,Didier Schwab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BenCSSmark基准，旨在将社会科学任务纳入大型语言模型（LLM）评估体系，以提升模型泛化性、鲁棒性及社会相关性，并促进AI与社会科学的协同研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流LLM基准严重缺乏社会科学任务，限制了LLM评估的全面性与社会科学研究的AI赋能潜力；而社会科学领域已积累大量高质量、情境敏感的标注数据，亟待整合进基准设计。

Method: 提出并构建BenCSSmark基准，该基准由计算社会科学学者标注的数据集组成，强调社会视角融入模型评估框架。

Result: BenCSSmark为LLM提供了面向历史、社会学、政治学、经济学等学科的社会科学任务评估能力，有望提升模型在真实社会语境下的表现与可解释性。

Conclusion: 将社会科学深度融入LLM基准建设，不仅可增强模型鲁棒性与适用性，还能推动AI系统更透明、更具社会意义，并促成跨学科高效协作。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the under-representation of social science tasks in contemporary LLM benchmarks limits advances in both LLM evaluation and social scientific inquiry. Benchmarks -- standardized tools for assessing computational systems -- are pivotal in the development of artificial intelligence (AI), including large language models (LLMs). Benchmarks do more than measure progress -- they actively structure it, shaping reputations, research agendas, and commercial outcomes. Despite this central role, the social sciences are largely absent from mainstream evaluation frameworks, even though scholars in these fields generate dozens of rigorously annotated, context-sensitive datasets each year. Integrating this work into benchmark design could significantly improve the generalization and robustness of AI models. In turn, models trained on social scientific tasks would likely yield better performance on classic and contemporary tasks in disciplines as diverse as history, sociology, political science or economics. This is all the more pressing as these disciplines are quickly turning to LLMs for assistance. To address this gap, we introduce BenCSSmark, a benchmark composed of datasets annotated by computational social scientists. By integrating social scientific perspectives into benchmarking, BenCSSmark seeks to promote more robust, transparent, and socially relevant AI systems and to foster efficient collaboration.

</details>


### [41] [Sentiment Analysis and Customer Satisfaction Prediction on E-Commerce Platforms Based on YouTube Comments Using the XGBoost Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04887)
*Ridho Benedictus Togi Manik,Muhammad Aqil Ramadhan,Ihsan Maulana Yusuf,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin Clinton Tosima Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用XGBoost与TF-IDF构建预测模型，分析印尼电商YouTube评论中的顾客满意度，并发现社会政治术语显著影响情感极性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印尼数字商务快速增长，消费者互动转向以视频为中心的社交网络（尤其是YouTube），导致大量非结构化、多语境评论难以人工进行情感追踪。

Method: 采用Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) 架构结合TF-IDF向量化，对从电商评测视频中获取的YouTube评论二级数据集进行文本预处理和特征工程，并使用PyCaret框架优化模型。

Result: PyCaret优化的机器学习框架展现出优异的分类鲁棒性；词法评估与特征重要性分析揭示电商话语中大量渗入社会政治术语，并显著影响观众满意度的情感极性。

Conclusion: 社会政治语义渗透是影响印尼电商评论情感分析的关键隐性因素，需在建模中加以识别与控制。

Abstract: The exponential expansion of digital commerce in Indonesia has significantly shifted consumer interactions toward video-centric social networks, particularly YouTube. Consequently, the sheer volume of unstructured, multi-contextual comments poses a tremendous challenge for manual sentiment tracking. This study investigates and constructs a predictive model for customer satisfaction leveraging the Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) architecture coupled with Term Frequency-Inverse Document Frequency (TF-IDF) vectorization. By utilizing a secondary dataset of YouTube comments retrieved from e-commerce review videos, the raw text underwent rigorous preprocessing to generate normalized numerical features. The experimental results demonstrate that the PyCaret-optimized machine learning framework delivers superior classification resilience. Beyond standard performance metrics, lexical evaluations and feature-importance mapping uncover a notable phenomenon: e-commerce discourse is heavily infiltrated by socio-political terminologies, which ultimately influence the polarity of audience satisfaction.

</details>

*Vita Anggraini,Cintya Bella,Bastian,Luluk Muthoharoh,Ardika Satria,Martin C. T. Manullang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了逻辑回归（TF-IDF特征）与双向LSTM在Sentiment140子集上的情感分析性能，发现传统方法（73.5%准确率）优于深度学习模型（69.17%），且后者存在轻微过拟合；最终模型通过Streamlit构建为可公开访问的Web应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体数据激增，亟需实时、自动化的非结构化公众情感分析系统。

Method: 对比实验：逻辑回归（TF-IDF特征） vs. 双向LSTM；使用Sentiment140数据集的10,000条推文子集；模型部署于Hugging Face Spaces，前端采用Streamlit。

Result: 逻辑回归准确率达73.5%，高于BiLSTM的69.17%；BiLSTM出现轻微过拟合；验证了经典机器学习在中等规模非正式文本上的有效性。

Conclusion: 对于中等规模的非正式文本（如推文），经过良好特征工程的传统机器学习方法可能比复杂深度学习模型更有效、更稳健。

Abstract: The exponential growth of social media has created an urgent need for automated systems to analyze unstructured public sentiment in real time. This study compares a traditional Logistic Regression model using TF-IDF features with a deep learning Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) architecture on a 10,000-tweet subset of the Sentiment140 dataset. Experimental results show that Logistic Regression outperformed BiLSTM, achieving an accuracy of 73.5% compared with 69.17%, while the deep learning model exhibited mild overfitting. These findings suggest that for medium-scale informal text data, classical machine learning with robust feature extraction can outperform more complex deep learning approaches. Finally, the trained models were integrated into an interactive web application using Streamlit and deployed on Hugging Face Spaces for public access.

</details>


### [43] [Storage Is Not Memory: A Retrieval-Centered Architecture for Agent Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04897)
*Joshua Adler,Guy Zehavi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出True Memory架构，通过多阶段检索管道替代传统提取式记忆存储，显著提升长上下文记忆检索准确率，在多个基准测试中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统记忆系统在数据摄入时即进行内容提取，导致查询未知前的信息丢失，无法在检索时恢复，因此需要一种能保留原始事件并支持按需检索的新架构。

Method: 提出六层True Memory架构，以多阶段检索流水线为核心，所有事件以原始形式保存，整个系统仅依赖单个SQLite文件运行，无需外部数据库、向量索引、图数据库或GPU。

Result: 在LoCoMo、LongMemEval和BEAM-1M三个基准上，True Memory Pro分别达到93.0%、87.8%和76.6%的准确率，均优于Mem0、Supermemory、Zep等主流方法，并略低于EverMemOS；56组消融实验显示最优配置族性能波动仅1.3个百分点。

Conclusion: True Memory证明了以检索为中心、保留原始事件的记忆架构在准确性与系统简洁性上的优越性，为Agent记忆系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Extraction at ingestion is the wrong primitive for agent memory: content discarded before the query is known cannot be recovered at retrieval time. We propose True Memory, a six-layer architecture that shifts the center of the system from a storage schema to a multi-stage retrieval pipeline operating over events preserved verbatim. The full system runs as a single SQLite file on commodity CPU with no external database, vector index, graph store, or GPU. On LoCoMo (1,540 questions across 10 multi-session conversations), True Memory Pro reaches 93.0% accuracy (3-run mean) against 61.4% for Mem0, 65.4% for Supermemory, approximately 71% for Zep, and 94.5% for EverMemOS under a matched gpt-4.1-mini answer model. On LongMemEval (500 questions), True Memory Pro reaches 87.8% (3-run mean). On BEAM-1M (700 questions at the 1-million-token scale), True Memory Pro reaches 76.6% (3-run mean), above the prior published result of 73.9% for Hindsight. A 56-configuration ablation shows a 1.3-percentage-point spread within the top-performing configuration family.

</details>


### [44] [Rethinking Local Learning: A Cheaper and Faster Recipe for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04913)
*Hengyu Shi,Tianyang Han,Peizhe Wang,Zhiling Wang,Xu Yang,Junhao Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LoPT（Local-Learning Post-Training），一种在Transformer中点设置梯度边界的局部学习后训练方法，使后半部分直接受任务梯度更新、前半部分通过轻量特征重建目标更新，从而降低内存开销、提升训练效率并更好保留预训练能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM后训练需全深度反向传播，导致高内存占用、长程依赖及对早期层表示的过度干扰，尤其当后训练监督信号远窄于预训练时，这种耦合代价高昂且不必要。

Method: LoPT在Transformer中间位置设置单一梯度边界：后半段模块直接优化任务目标；前半段模块则通过轻量级特征重建目标进行更新，以保持表征质量和层间接口兼容性。

Result: LoPT在多个任务上达到与全参数微调相当的性能，同时显著降低显存消耗、加快训练速度，并更好保留预训练模型的语言与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 局部学习后训练是一种更高效、更少侵入性的LLM适配范式，验证了将梯度传播范围作为显式设计选择的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: LLM post-training typically propagates task gradients through the full depth of the model. Although this end-to-end structure is simple and general, it couples task adaptation to full-depth activation storage, long-range backward dependencies and direct task-gradient access to pretrained representations. We argue that this full-depth backward coupling can be unnecessarily expensive and intrusive, particularly when post-training supervision is much narrower than pre-training. To this end, we propose \textbf{LoPT}: Local-Learning Post-Training, a simple post-training strategy that makes gradient reach an explicit design choice. LoPT places a single gradient boundary at the transformer midpoint: the second-half block learns from the task objective, while the first-half block is updated by a lightweight feature-reconstruction objective to preserve useful representations and maintain interface compatibility. LoPT shortens the task-induced backward path while limiting direct interference from narrow task gradients on early-layer representations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LoPT achieves competitive performance with lower memory cost, higher training efficiency and better retention of pretrained capabilities. Our code is available at: https://github.com/HumyuShi/LoPT

</details>


### [45] [Unintended Negative Impacts of Promotional Language in Patent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04926)
*Bingkun Zhao,Chenwei Zhang,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究发现，在专利申请中使用过多宣传性语言会降低专利授权、所有权转让和上诉成功的概率，且这种‘宣传惩罚’在不同技术领域普遍存在；但宣传性语言并非掩盖技术薄弱的伪装，反而客观反映了组合新颖性和未来引用影响力；此外，审查员的性别和经验显著影响其对宣传性语言的容忍度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究宣传性语言在技术专利评估中的作用，弥补其在科技创新语境下研究不足的问题。

Method: 基于270万份美国专利商标局（USPTO）专利申请数据，使用经验证和领域校准的135个宣传性词汇词典，分析宣传性语言频率与专利授权、转让及上诉成功等评估结果之间的关联，并控制混杂变量、进行匹配样本分析及机制检验。

Result: （1）宣传性语言频率越高，专利被授权、转让及上诉成功的概率越低，该效应稳健且跨技术领域存在；（2）宣传性语言并非弱技术的掩饰，而是与组合新颖性和未来引用影响力正相关；（3）男性和资深审查员比女性和新手审查员更宽容宣传性表述。

Conclusion: 专利系统中存在宣传性语言使用的悖论：它既反映真实创新潜力，又招致制度性惩罚；研究呼吁通过更客观地审视专利文本语言模式来改进专利评估实践。

Abstract: Promotional language has been increasingly used to aid the communication of innovative ideas in science. Yet, less is known about its role in the context of technological innovation. Here, we use a validated and domain-diagnosed lexicon of 135 promotional words to study the association between promotional language and patent evaluation outcomes among 2.7 million USPTO patent applications. Our large-scale study reveals three unexpected findings. First, in contrast to scientific evaluation, we find that a higher frequency of promotional words is negatively associated with the probability of an application being (i) granted a patent, (ii) transferred ownership, and (iii) successfully appealed. This promotional penalty holds even after accounting for a range of confounding factors and is largely robust across different technological areas. Among matched samples, the difference in the success rate between the lowest and highest promotional density quintile is 5.5, 5.9, and 5.3 percentage points for patentability, transferability, and rejection reversal. Second, contrary to institutional skepticism, we show that promotional language is not a mask of weak technology, but objectively reflects the degree of combinatorial novelty and future citation impact. Third, digging into the mechanisms, we find that the tolerance to promotional framing is strongly moderated by human factors, with men and experienced examiners showing a higher acceptance of promotional narratives than women and novice examiners. By revealing an emerging paradox in the patent system, our study offers theoretical and practical implications for improving patent evaluation through more objective scrutiny of linguistic patterns in patent filings.

</details>

Abstract: This paper describes our system submitted to SemEval-2026 Task 11: Disentangling Content and Formal Reasoning in Large Language Models. We present an efficient modular neuro-symbolic approach, combining a symbolic prover with small reasoning LLMs (4B parameters). The system consists of an LLM-based parser that translates natural language syllogisms to a first-order logic (FOL) representation, an automated theorem prover, and two optional modules: machine translation for multilingual inputs and a symbolic retrieval component for the identification of relevant premises. The system achieves competitive accuracy and relatively low content effect on most subtasks. Our ablations show that this approach outperforms LLM-based zero-shot baselines in this parameter size range, but also reveal limited multilingual capabilities of small LLMs. Finally, we include a discussion of the task's main ranking metric and analyze its limitations.

</details>

Conclusion: QLoRA在7B级模型上为巴什基尔语适配提供了质量与效率的良好折中；但PEFT效果高度依赖基础模型与分词器匹配，不能一概而论；困惑度并非生成连贯性与语言纯度的可靠代理指标。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, including LoRA and QLoRA, applied to the task of adapting large language models to the Bashkir language, a low-resource agglutinative language of the Turkic family. Experimental evaluation is conducted on a Bashkir text corpus of 71k documents (46.9M tokens) using models of various architectures: DistilGPT2, GPT-2 (base, medium), Phi-2, Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B, and Mistral-7B. To improve the reliability of results, each configuration was trained with three different random seeds.
  The lowest perplexity on the test set was obtained for GPT-2 medium with full fine-tuning (3.34). Meanwhile, QLoRA applied to Mistral-7B (3.79) and Phi-2 (3.81) achieved comparable quality with over 40 times fewer trainable parameters. However, we also observed cases of significant quality degradation when using PEFT for certain architectures (e.g., DeepSeek-7B with rank 8, perplexity = 129.55), indicating that the outcome depends critically on the choice of the base model and its tokenizer.
  Additionally, a qualitative analysis of generated texts based on Bashkir prompts revealed that models with the best perplexity do not necessarily produce the most coherent outputs: QLoRA-tuned models generated monolingual Bashkir continuations, whereas the fully fine-tuned model with the lowest perplexity frequently switched to English. The results suggest that QLoRA on 7B-scale models offers an effective compromise between quality and computational cost for Bashkir. To ensure reproducibility, open data, code, and trained adapters will be released upon acceptance.

</details>

*Minjie Qiang,Mingming Zhang,Xiaoyi Bao,Xing Fu,Yu Cheng,Weiqiang Wang,Zhongqing Wang,Ningtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TabBench基准和首个通用表格嵌入模型TabEmbed，通过对比学习与正样本感知的难负例挖掘，统一表格分类与检索任务，在表格表示学习中取得新基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Foundation models have established unified representations for natural language processing, yet this paradigm remains largely unexplored for tabular data. Existing methods face fundamental limitations: LLM-based approaches lack retrieval-compatible vector outputs, whereas text embedding models often fail to capture tabular structure and numerical semantics. To bridge this gap, we first introduce the Tabular Embedding Benchmark (TabBench), a comprehensive suite designed to evaluate the tabular understanding capability of embedding models. We then propose TabEmbed, the first generalist embedding model that unifies tabular classification and retrieval within a shared embedding space. By reformulating diverse tabular tasks as semantic matching problems, TabEmbed leverages large-scale contrastive learning with positive-aware hard negative mining to discern fine-grained structural and numerical nuances. Experimental results on TabBench demonstrate that TabEmbed significantly outperforms state-of-the-art text embedding models, establishing a new baseline for universal tabular representation learning. Code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/qiangminjie27/TabEmbed and https://huggingface.co/datasets/qiangminjie27/TabBench.

</details>


### [49] [Why Expert Alignment Is Hard: Evidence from Subjective Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04972)
*Tzu-Mi Lin,Wataru Hirota,Tatsuya Ishigaki,Lung-Hao Lee,Chung-Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型与专家判断对齐的困难，特别是在主观评估任务中。通过专家评估和后续问卷调查，作者发现专家对齐难度因人而异、显式标准未必提升对齐效果、少量编辑效果不稳定但有用，且不同评估维度对齐难度差异显著。结果表明，对齐困难不仅源于模型局限，更源于主观评估本身的异质性、部分默会性、维度依赖性和时间不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主观评估任务中，专家可能意见不一、依赖默会标准并随时间改变判断，导致大模型与专家判断对齐尤为困难；本文旨在通过研究专家对齐来理解这一困难的本质。

Method: 采用专家评估数据与后续问卷调查，系统分析不同形式的专家信息（如显式标准、推理过程、编辑示例等）对模型对齐效果的影响，并按评估维度分类比较。

Result: 发现四个一致模式：（1）不同专家对齐难度差异大；（2）显式标准和推理不一定改善对齐；（3）编辑效果对示例数量和身份敏感，少量编辑带来有用但不稳定的提升；（4）内容直接相关的评估维度较易对齐，需外部知识或价值判断的维度更难对齐。

Conclusion: 专家对齐困难不仅源于模型能力限制，更深层原因在于主观评估本身具有异质性、部分默会性、维度依赖性和时间不稳定性。

Abstract: Aligning large language models with expert judgment is especially difficult in subjective evaluation tasks, where experts may disagree, rely on tacit criteria, and change their judgments over time. In this paper, we study expert alignment as a way to understand this difficulty. Using expert evaluations and follow-up questionnaires, we examine how different forms of expert information affect alignment and what this reveals about subjective judgment. Our findings show four consistent patterns. First, alignment difficulty varies substantially across experts, suggesting that expert evaluation styles differ widely in their distance from a model's prior behavior. Second, explicit criteria and reasoning do not always improve alignment, indicating that expert judgment is not fully captured by verbalized rules. Third, editing is sensitive to both the number and the identity of examples, with small numbers of edits providing useful but unstable gains. Fourth, alignment difficulty differs across evaluation dimensions: dimensions grounded more directly in proposal content are easier to align, while dimensions requiring external knowledge or value-based judgment remain harder. Taken together, these results suggest that expert alignment is difficult not only because of model limitations, but also because subjective evaluation is inherently heterogeneous, partly tacit, dimension-dependent, and temporally unstable.

</details>


### [50] [Misaligned by Reward: Socially Undesirable Preferences in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05003)
*Gayane Ghazaryan,Esra Dönmez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文扩展了奖励模型的基准测试，涵盖偏见、安全性、道德和伦理推理四个社会关键领域，发现现有模型在社会对齐方面存在显著缺陷，并揭示了避免偏见与保持上下文忠实性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励模型评估主要集中在指令遵循等宽泛基准上，难以揭示其在社会可取偏好（如公平、安全、道德）上的缺陷，导致重要社会对齐失败被掩盖。

Method: 提出一个将社会评估数据集转化为成对偏好数据的框架，利用已有黄金标签或方向性偏差指示器；在五种公开奖励模型和两种用作奖励代理的指令微调模型上进行跨领域评估。

Result: 各模型在不同社会领域表现差异大，无一全面最优；普遍存在偏好社会不良选项、输出分布系统性偏差等问题；更强的偏差规避能力常以牺牲上下文敏感性为代价。

Conclusion: 标准奖励基准不足以评估社会对齐，亟需直接衡量奖励模型所编码社会偏好的新评估范式。

Abstract: Reward models are a key component of large language model alignment, serving as proxies for human preferences during training. However, existing evaluations focus primarily on broad instruction-following benchmarks, providing limited insight into whether these models capture socially desirable preferences. As a result, important failures in social alignment can remain hidden.
  We extend reward-model benchmarking to four socially consequential domains: bias, safety, morality, and ethical reasoning. We introduce a framework that converts social evaluation datasets into pairwise preference data, leveraging gold labels where available and directional bias indicators otherwise. This enables us to test whether reward models prefer socially undesirable responses, and whether their preferences produce systematically biased distributions over selected outputs.
  Across five publicly available reward models and two instruction-tuned models used as reward proxies, we find substantial variation across domains, with no single model performing best overall. The models fall well short of strong social intelligence: they often prefer socially undesirable options, and their preferences produce systematically biased distributions. Moreover, stronger bias avoidance can reduce sensitivity to context, revealing a key alignment trade-off between avoiding biased outcomes and preserving contextual faithfulness. These findings show that standard reward benchmarks are insufficient for assessing social alignment and highlight the need for evaluations that directly measure the social preferences encoded in reward models.

</details>


### [51] [Detecting Hallucinations in Large Language Models via Internal Attention Divergence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05025)
*Gijs van Dijk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、单次前向传播的不确定性量化方法，利用注意力矩阵估计大语言模型中的幻觉，无需重复采样或外部模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测大语言模型（LLM）生成内容中的幻觉（hallucination），亟需高效、可解释且不依赖额外计算开销的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 基于各注意力头输出分布与均匀参考分布之间的KL散度构建不确定性特征，并用逻辑回归探针进行二分类（正确/错误回答）；该方法仅需单次前向传播，完全基于模型内部注意力机制（白盒）。

Result: 在多个数据集、任务类型和模型家族上验证，注意力散度信号对答案正确性具有高预测性，性能媲美现有不确定性估计方法；且该信号主要集中于中间层及事实性词元（如命名实体、数字）。

Conclusion: 注意力动态本身即提供一种高效、可解释的白盒不确定性信号，为LLM可靠性评估提供了新思路。

Abstract: We propose a lightweight and single-pass uncertainty quantification method for detecting hallucinations in Large Language Models. The method uses attention matrices to estimate uncertainty without requiring repeated sampling or external models. Specifically, we measure the Kullback-Leibler divergence between each attention head's distribution and a uniform reference distribution, and use these features in a logistic regression probe. Across multiple datasets, task types, and model families, attention divergence is highly predictive of answer correctness and performs competitively with existing uncertainty estimation methods. We find that this signal is concentrated in middle layers and on factual tokens such as named entities and numbers, suggesting that attention dynamics provides an efficient and interpretable white-box signal of model uncertainty.

</details>


### [52] [The Impossibility Triangle of Long-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05066)
*Yan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了长序列模型中效率、紧凑性和回忆能力三者之间存在根本性权衡，无法同时满足；通过在线序列处理器抽象和信息论工具证明了理论界限，并在52种架构和实验中验证了该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解长序列建模中计算效率、状态大小与历史信息回忆能力之间的基本限制。

Method: 提出在线序列处理器抽象框架，结合数据处理不等式和Fano不等式进行理论证明，并对52种模型进行分类分析，辅以合成关联回忆任务实验验证。

Result: 证明任意满足效率与紧凑性的模型其回忆容量至多为O(poly(d)/log V)；所有测试架构均落于三者不可兼得的三角形内，无一例外。

Conclusion: 长序列模型必然面临效率、紧凑性与回忆能力的三元权衡，该结论具有普适性，适用于Transformer、状态空间模型、线性循环网络及其混合体。

Abstract: We identify and prove a fundamental trade-off governing long-sequence models: no model can simultaneously achieve (i) per-step computation independent of sequence length (Efficiency), (ii) state size independent of sequence length (Compactness), and (iii) the ability to recall a number of historical facts proportional to sequence length (Recall). We formalize this trade-off within an Online Sequence Processor abstraction that unifies Transformers, state space models, linear recurrent networks, and their hybrids. Using the Data Processing Inequality and Fano's Inequality, we prove that any model satisfying Efficiency and Compactness can recall at most O(poly(d)/log V) key-value pairs from a sequence of arbitrary length, where d is the model dimension and V is the vocabulary size. We classify 52 architectures published before March 2026 into the triangle, showing that each achieves at most two of the three properties and that hybrid architectures trace continuous trajectories in the interior. Experiments on synthetic associative recall tasks with five representative architectures validate the theoretical bound: empirical recall capacity lies strictly below the information-theoretic limit, and no architecture escapes the triangle.

</details>


### [53] [The Pinocchio Dimension: Phenomenality of Experience as the Primary Axis of LLM Psychometric Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05080)
*Hubert Plisiecki,Sabina Siudaj,Kacper Dudzic,Anna Sterna,Maciej Gorski,Karolina Drozdz,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过向50个大语言模型（LLMs）施测45种心理测量问卷，发现模型间主要的心理测量差异维度是“匹诺曹轴（Pinocchio Axis）”——即模型将自身表征为现象经验主体（如具身感受、情绪、内心言语等）还是行为反应系统；该轴由新提出的无标注指标Pinocchio分数（π_i）刻画，并证实其受训练（尤其是后训练微调）塑造的自我表征倾向驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在心理测量维度上的系统性差异，特别是是否存在超越传统人格特质的、反映其自我表征本质的深层结构。

Method: 对50个LLM施测45个验证过的心理量表；采用监督语义差异法（SSD）识别主变异轴；提出无标注的Pinocchio分数（π_i）量化各题项的经验需求；用主成分分析（PCA）提取跨量表的模型得分主导维度（即Pinocchio轴Π）；检验Π与π_i及模型训练路径的关系。

Result: 发现一个主导的跨模型变异轴——Pinocchio轴（Π），解释47.1%的方差，高度关联Pinocchio分数（r=.864）；该轴分离‘现象经验描述’与‘刺激驱动行为反应’类题项；Pinocchio分数能预测题项在条件提示下的因子载荷变化；同一厂商不同模型间显著分化，支持后训练微调塑造该自我表征倾向。

Conclusion: LLM间最主要的心理测量差异并非传统人格维度，而是一种训练塑造的自我表征立场——即模型在多大程度上将体验性语言自我指涉化，表现为‘作为经验主体’还是‘作为行为系统’的倾向。

Abstract: We administer 45 validated psychometric questionnaires to 50 large language models (LLMs) to identify the dimensions along which LLMs differ psychometrically. Using Supervised Semantic Differential (SSD), we find that the primary axis of between-model variance separates items describing phenomenally rich experience, including embodied sensation, felt affect, inner speech, imagery, and empathy, from items describing stimulus-driven behavioral reactivity ($R^2_{adj}=.037$, $p<.0001$). To test this hypothesis at the item level, we introduce the Pinocchio score ($π_i$), the ratio of inter-model response variance under neutral prompting to that under a human-simulation prompt, as an annotation-free measure of each item's experiential demand. $π_i$ predicts condition-induced shifts in primary factor loading magnitudes ($ρ=-.215$, $p<.0001$, $n=1292$--$1310$ items), confirming that between-model divergence on experiential items is structured rather than noisy. Applying PCA to per-model EFA scores across all questionnaires reveals one dominant dimension, the Pinocchio Axis ($Π$): the degree to which a model presents itself as a locus of phenomenal experience rather than a system of behavioral responses. This axis captures 47.1% of cross-questionnaire between-model variance in primary factor scores and converges with item-level Pinocchio scores ($r=.864$). Marked within-provider divergence across closely related model variants is consistent with post-training fine-tuning as a key contributor, supporting the interpretation that $Π$ reflects a training-shaped self-representational tendency governing how a model treats experiential language as self-applicable. The dominant axis of between-model psychometric variation is therefore not a conventional personality trait but a self-representational stance toward one's own nature as an experiencer.

</details>


### [54] [Automatically Finding and Validating Unexpected Side-Effects of Interventions on Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05090)
*Quintin Pope,Ajay Hayagreeve Balaji,Jacques Thibodeau,Xiaoli Fern*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化的对比评估流程，用于审计干预措施对大语言模型行为影响，通过比较基础模型与干预模型在对齐提示下的生成差异，生成可解释、统计验证的自然语言假设，并在合成和真实场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏一种自动化、可解释且统计上可靠的工具来审计干预措施对大语言模型行为的实际影响。

Method: 构建对比评估流水线：在对齐提示下对比基础模型M1与干预模型M2的自由形式多词元生成，利用统计检验识别显著差异，并归纳生成自然语言假设与跨假设的主题模式。

Result: 在合成数据中能可靠恢复预设行为变化；在推理蒸馏、知识编辑和遗忘三个真实干预任务中，成功揭示预期与意外行为变化，区分强弱干预，并避免无效应或错配提示下的虚假差异。

Conclusion: 该流水线为干预后模型行为审计提供了一种统计严谨、结果可解释的实用工具。

Abstract: We present an automated, contrastive evaluation pipeline for auditing the behavioral impact of interventions on large language models. Given a base model $M_1$ and an intervention model $M_2$, our method compares their free-form, multi-token generations across aligned prompt contexts and produces human-readable, statistically validated natural-language hypotheses describing how the models differ, along with recurring themes that summarize patterns across validated hypotheses.
  We evaluate the approach in synthetic setting by injecting known behavioral changes and showing that the pipeline reliably recovers them. We then apply it to three real-world interventions, reasoning distillation, knowledge editing and unlearning, demonstrating that the method surfaces both intended and unexpected behavioral shifts, distinguishes large from subtle interventions, and does not hallucinate differences when effects are absent or misaligned with the prompt bank. Overall, the pipeline provides a statistically grounded and interpretable tool for post-hoc auditing of intervention-induced changes in model behavior.

</details>


### [55] [Text Corpora as Concept Fields: Black-Box Hallucination and Novelty Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05103)
*Nicholas S. Kersting,Vittorio Castelli,Chieh Ting Yeh,Xinzhu Wang,Saad Taame*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为'概念场（Concept Field）'的新方法，用于在句子嵌入空间中建模文本语义的局部漂移与不确定性，以检测幻觉（hallucination）和新颖性（novelty），具有黑箱、可归因、概率可解释等特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM检测方法（如检索基线或白盒模型）缺乏跨域一致的概率解释能力，且难以兼顾可解释性与轻量性；需要一种不依赖模型内部结构、能从语料本身直接导出、并支持概率解读的语义一致性度量。

Method: 构建'概念场'：在句子嵌入空间中，基于相邻句向量差（delta）估计局部高斯漂移场，并定义ζ得分（观测delta与场估计间的平均绝对z距离）；配套引入向量序列数据库（VSDB）存储嵌入、位置及delta元数据；进一步探索概念场的散度与旋度以揭示语义源/汇与隐含主题。

Result: 在联邦法规（接地性检测）与古腾堡项目（新颖性检测）两个大规模任务上，Concept Fields在'接地/未接地/不确定'三类分类中展现出强选择性性能；其覆盖-风险行为跨域稳定，支持概率迁移解释；散度/旋度分析初步揭示了有意义的语义结构。

Conclusion: Concept Fields是一种快速、轻量、可解释的新型语义信号，可作为LLM-as-judge和白盒检测器的互补工具，在 groundedness 与 novelty 检测中兼具实用性与理论启发性。

Abstract: We introduce the **Concept Field** of a text corpus: a local drift field with pointwise uncertainty, estimated in sentence-embedding space from the deltas between consecutive sentences. Given a candidate sentence transition, we score its agreement with the field by $ζ$, the mean absolute z-distance between the observed delta and the field's local Gaussian estimate. The score is black-box (no model internals), corpus-attributable (every score traces to nearby corpus sentences), and admits a direct probabilistic reading. We support the computation with the introduction of a **Vector Sequence Database (VSDB)** that stores embeddings together with sequence-position and next-delta metadata. We evaluate this approach on two large-scale settings: hallucination-style groundedness detection over the U.S. Code of Federal Regulations, and novelty detection over Project Gutenberg. Using controlled LLM-generated rewrites, Concept Fields achieve strong selective classification performance under a grounded / ungrounded / unsure triage policy, which unlike retrieval-centric baselines have similar coverage-risk behavior across both domains, supporting a probability-based interpretation that transfers across domains. We also sketch how divergence and curl of the Concept Field, computed on dense clusters, surface qualitatively meaningful semantic patterns (logic sources, sinks, and implicit topics), which we offer as hypothesis-generating rather than as a quantitative result. Concept Fields provide a fast, lightweight, and interpretable signal for groundedness and novelty, complementary to LLM-as-judge and white-box detectors.

</details>


### [56] [Beyond Semantics: An Evidential Reasoning-Aware Multi-View Learning Framework for Trustworthy Mental Health Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05121)
*Yucheng Ruan,Ling Huang,Qika Lin,Kai He,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多视角学习框架，结合编码器和解码器模型的语义与推理信息，并基于主观逻辑的证据学习方法建模不确定性，以提升心理健康预测的可靠性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的心理健康预测模型在真实高风险场景中面临过自信预测、缺乏可靠不确定性估计等问题，难以满足风险敏感型应用需求。

Method: 将任务建模为多视角学习问题，融合encoder-only模型的语义表征与decoder-only模型的推理表征；引入基于主观逻辑（Subjective Logic）的证据学习框架进行不确定性建模，并设计证据融合策略以平衡互补视角并抑制不可靠证据。

Result: 在Dreaddit、SDCNL和DepSeverity三个真实数据集上分别达到0.835、0.731和0.751的准确率；鲁棒性与可解释性实验表明该方法兼具更高性能、可信不确定性估计及人类可理解的推理信号。

Conclusion: 所提框架显著提升了心理健康预测模型的可靠性、鲁棒性与可解释性，适用于临床辅助决策等风险敏感场景。

Abstract: Automated mental health prediction using textual data has shown promising results with deep learning and large language models. However, deploying these models in high-stakes real-world settings remains challenging, as existing approaches largely rely on semantic representations and often produce overconfident predictions under ambiguous, noisy, or shifted data. Moreover, most methods lack reliable uncertainty estimation, undermining trust in risk-sensitive mental health applications. To address these limitations, we formulate the task as a multi-view learning problem that integrates semantic information from encoder-only models with higher-level reasoning information from decoder-only models, where reasoning-aware representations and uncertainty modeling are obtained in a trustworthy manner. To ensure reliable fusion, we adopt an evidential learning framework based on Subjective Logic to explicitly model uncertainty and introduce an evidential fusion strategy that balances complementary views while discounting unreliable evidence. Benchmarking on three real-world datasets, Dreaddit, SDCNL, and DepSeverity, reports accuracies of 0.835, 0.731, and 0.751, respectively, demonstrating its potential for reliable mental health prediction. Additional experiments on robustness to noise and case studies for interpretability confirm that our proposed framework not only improves predictive performance but also provides trustworthy uncertainty estimates and human-understandable reasoning signals, making it suitable for risk-sensitive applications in mental health assessment.

</details>


### [57] [PSK at SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection Using Ensemble Gemma Models with Synthetic Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05159)
*Srikar Kashyap Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对22语种的多语言极化检测方法，基于LoRA微调多尺寸Gemma模型，并结合GPT-4o-mini生成的合成数据与多阶段质量过滤；通过单语阈值调优和加权集成提升性能，最终在SemEval-2026 Task 9中获得总榜第二、3个语种第一、8个语种前三的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多语言极化检测任务中跨语言泛化能力弱、标注数据稀缺的问题，尤其应对22种语言的统一建模挑战。

Method: 对每种语言分别微调Gemma-12B/27B模型，采用LoRA高效适配；使用GPT-4o-mini实施三种合成数据策略（直接生成、改写、对比对构建），并经嵌入去重等多阶段过滤；引入单语阈值调优与12B/27B模型加权集成，并按语言选择最优合成策略。

Result: 在22种语言上平均macro-F1达0.811，总排名第二；其中3种语言第一、8种语言进入前三；XLM-RoBERTa与Qwen3等替代模型在测试集上F1骤降30–50%，凸显泛化重要性。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型的合成数据增强与轻量微调策略能有效提升多语言极化检测性能，但模型选择与泛化评估需谨慎；单语精细化调优（如阈值、策略、集成）比统一架构更关键。

Abstract: We present our system for SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection, a binary classification task spanning 22 languages. Our approach fine-tunes separate Gemma~3 models (12B and 27B parameters) per language using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), augmented with synthetic data generated by a large language model (LLM). We employ three synthetic data strategies (direct generation, paraphrasing, and contrastive pair creation) using GPT-4o-mini, with a multi-stage quality filtering pipeline including embedding-based deduplication. We find that per-language threshold tuning on the development set yields 2 to 4\% F1 improvements without retraining. We also use weighted ensembles of 12B and 27B model predictions with per-language strategy selection. Our final system achieves a mean macro-F1 of 0.811 across all 22 languages, ranking 2nd overall of the participating teams, with 1st place finishes in 3 languages and top-3 in 8 languages. We also find that alternative architectures (XLM-RoBERTa, Qwen3) that showed strong development set performance suffered 30 to 50\% F1 drops on the test set, highlighting the importance of generalization.

</details>


### [58] [The First Token Knows: Single-Decode Confidence for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05166)
*Mina Gabriel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本的幻觉检测指标phi_first，仅需单次贪婪解码的第一个语义相关token的top-K logits归一化熵，即可在事实性问答任务上媲美甚至超越多采样语义一致性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自一致性（self-consistency）方法依赖多次采样，计算开销大且对词汇变化敏感；语义自一致性虽改进了语义层面的一致性判断，但引入了额外的采样和自然语言推理开销。因此需要一种更轻量、高效且信息丰富的不确定性估计方法。

Method: 提出phi_first指标：基于单次贪婪解码，在第一个内容承载答案token处，计算top-K logits的归一化熵作为不确定性度量；在三个7-8B指令微调模型和两个基准上评估其AUROC性能，并与语义自一致性及表面形式自一致性对比；进行子集包含性检验以分析phi_first与语义一致性的相关性。

Result: phi_first在三个7-8B模型和两个基准上的平均AUROC达0.820，优于语义自一致性（0.793）和标准自一致性（0.791）；phi_first与语义一致性呈中至强相关，二者联合仅带来微小提升；表明模型首token分布已蕴含大量不确定性信息。

Conclusion: phi_first是一种高效、低开销且信息丰富的幻觉检测基线指标，应作为采样类不确定性估计前的默认报告指标。

Abstract: Self-consistency detects hallucinations by generating multiple sampled answers to a question and measuring agreement, but this requires repeated decoding and can be sensitive to lexical variation. Semantic self-consistency improves this by clustering sampled answers by meaning using natural language inference, but it adds both sampling cost and external inference overhead. We show that first-token confidence, phi_first, computed from the normalized entropy of the top-K logits at the first content-bearing answer token of a single greedy decode, matches or modestly exceeds semantic self-consistency on closed-book short-answer factual question answering. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models and two benchmarks, phi_first achieves a mean AUROC of 0.820, compared with 0.793 for semantic agreement and 0.791 for standard surface-form self-consistency. A subsumption test shows that phi_first is moderately to strongly correlated with semantic agreement, and combining the two signals yields only a small AUROC improvement over phi_first alone. These results suggest that much of the uncertainty information captured by multi-sample agreement is already available in the model's initial token distribution. We argue that phi_first should be reported as a default low-cost baseline before invoking sampling-based uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [59] [Implicit Representations of Grammaticality in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05197)
*Yingshan Susan Wang,Linlu Qiu,Zhaofeng Wu,Roger P. Levy,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨预训练语言模型是否在内部表征中隐式习得了语法正确性判断能力，发现通过线性探针可有效提取该能力，其性能优于基于字符串概率的语法判断，并具有一定跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管语言模型以最大化语料似然为目标，但其字符串概率对语法正确性区分不明显；本文旨在探究模型是否在内部表征中隐式习得独立于似然的语法判断能力。

Method: 在人工构造的语法正确与错误句子数据集上训练线性探针，分析语言模型隐藏层表征，并在多个语法判断和语义合理性基准上评估其泛化能力及与字符串概率的相关性。

Result: 语法探针在多语言语法判断任务上优于字符串概率，在语义合理性任务上则不如；探针分数与字符串概率弱相关，且展现出跨语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: 语言模型确实在隐藏层中隐式习得了某种程度上独立于字符串概率的语法正确性区分能力。

Abstract: Grammaticality and likelihood are distinct notions in human language. Pretrained language models (LMs), which are probabilistic models of language fitted to maximize corpus likelihood, generate grammatically well-formed text and discriminate well between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences in tightly controlled minimal pairs. However, their string probabilities do not sharply discriminate between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences overall. But do LMs implicitly acquire a grammaticality distinction distinct from string probability? We explore this question through studying internal representations of LMs, by training a linear probe on a dataset of grammatical and (synthetic) ungrammatical sentences obtained by applying perturbations to a naturalistic text corpus. We find that this simple grammaticality probe generalizes to human-curated grammaticality judgment benchmarks and outperforms LM probability-based grammaticality judgments. When applied to semantic plausibility benchmarks, in which both members of a minimal pair are grammatical and differ in only plausibility, the probe however performs worse than string probability. The English-trained probe also exhibits nontrivial cross-lingual generalization, outperforming string probabilities on grammaticality benchmarks in numerous other languages. Additionally, probe scores correlate only weakly with string probabilities. These results collectively suggest that LMs acquire to some extent an implicit grammaticality distinction within their hidden layers.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [60] [Are Multimodal LLMs Ready for Clinical Dermatology? A Real-World Evaluation in Dermatology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04098)
*Roy Jiang,Hyunjae Kim,Zhenyue Qin,Morten Lee,Margaret MacGibeny,Ailish Hanly,Angela Sadlowski,Shanin Chowdhury,Xuguang Ai,Jeffrey Gehlhausen,Qingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了多个多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在公开皮肤病学数据集与真实临床会诊数据上的诊断与分诊性能，发现模型在真实场景中表现显著下降，表明当前基准测试结果严重高估了其临床实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有皮肤病学多模态大语言模型在公开基准上表现良好，但其是否适用于真实临床决策尚不明确；本文旨在量化‘基准到床边’（benchmark-to-bedside）的性能差距。

Method: 在三个公开皮肤病数据集和一个包含5,811例、46,405张临床图像的多中心回顾性医院会诊队列上，评估四款开源MLLM（InternVL-Chat v1.5、LLaVA-Med v1.5、SkinGPT4、MedGemma-4B-Instruct）及一款商用模型（GPT-4.1），任务为差分诊断生成与基于严重程度的分诊。

Result: 模型在公开数据集上top-3诊断准确率最高达42.25%（GPT-4.1），但在真实会诊图像中骤降至1.50%–24.65%；加入临床文本后提升至最高38.93%，但对错误/缺失上下文高度敏感；分诊任务敏感度>60%，但可靠性不足。

Conclusion: 当前皮肤病学MLLMs在真实临床环境中的性能远低于基准测试结果，尚不具备直接临床部署条件，亟需更贴近真实场景的评估范式与鲁棒性改进。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated promise on publicly available dermatology benchmarks. However, benchmark performance may not generalize to real-world dermatologic decision-making. To quantify this benchmark-to-bedside gap, we evaluated four open-weight MLLMs (InternVL-Chat v1.5, LLaVA-Med v1.5, SkinGPT4 and MedGemma-4B-Instruct) and one commercial MLLM (GPT-4.1) across three publicly available dermatology datasets and a retrospective multi-site hospital-based dermatology consultation cohort comprising 5,811 cases and 46,405 clinical images. Models were evaluated on two clinically relevant tasks: differential diagnosis generation and severity-based triage. Diagnostic performance was modest on public datasets and declined substantially in the real-world cohort. On public benchmarks, top-3 diagnostic accuracy reached 26.55% for the best open-weight model and 42.25% for GPT-4.1. On real-world consultation cases using images alone, top-3 diagnostic accuracy fell to 1.50%-13.35% among open-weight models and 24.65% for GPT-4.1. Incorporating clinical context improved performance across all models, increasing top-3 diagnostic accuracy up to 28.75% among open-weight models and 38.93% for GPT-4.1. However, model outputs were highly sensitive to incomplete or erroneous consultation context. For severity-based triage, models achieved moderate sensitivity (above 60%), suggesting potential utility for screening but insufficient reliability for clinical deployment. These findings demonstrate that benchmark performance substantially overestimates the real-world clinical capability of current dermatology MLLMs.

</details>


### [61] [MuCALD-SplitFed: Causal-Latent Diffusion for Privacy-Preserving Multi-Task Split-Federated Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04108)
*Chamani Shiranthika,Hadi Hadizadeh,Parvaneh Saeedi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MuCALD-SplitFed，一种结合因果表征学习与潜在扩散的多任务Split Federated Learning框架，用于解决医疗多机构异构任务下的模型训练不稳定与隐私泄露问题，在医学图像分割任务中显著提升性能并增强隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实医疗场景中，各机构任务各异，标准联邦学习与SplitFed难以适配；多任务FL虽可应对异构任务，但存在训练不稳定和隐私风险。

Method: 提出MuCALD-SplitFed框架，融合因果表示学习以解耦任务相关/无关特征，并引入潜在扩散模型增强客户端本地表征鲁棒性与隐私性；模型在客户端与服务器间分割，支持多任务协同训练。

Result: 在医学图像分割任务上，MuCALD-SplitFed稳定收敛且性能显著优于基线SplitFed（后者无法收敛）；有效降低分割点信息泄露，抵御重建攻击与成员推断攻击；整体表现超越现有个性化FL与多任务FL方法。

Conclusion: MuCALD-SplitFed为异构医疗多任务场景提供了兼顾性能、稳定性与隐私安全的SplitFed新范式。

Abstract: Federated Learning enables decentralized training by aggregating model updates across clients without sharing raw data, while Split Federated Learning further partitions the model between clients and a server to reduce computation and communication at the client side. However, decentralized medical institutions rarely operate on a single shared task, making standard Federated and SplitFed collaborations poorly aligned with real clinical workflows. Multi-task FL extends these frameworks by allowing clients to handle different tasks, but often introduces instability and privacy vulnerabilities. This study proposes \textbf{MuCALD-SplitFed}, a multi-task SplitFed framework that integrates causal representation learning and latent diffusion. Experiments show MuCALD-SplitFed consistently improves segmentation, while baseline SplitFed fails to converge. The proposed approach further reduces information leakage at split points, mitigating reconstruction-based and membership inference attacks. Additionally, MuCALD SplitFed outperforms state-of-the-art personalized FL and multi-task FL approaches. The code repository is: https://github.com/ChamaniS/MuCALD_SplitFed.

</details>


### [62] [Topology-Constrained Quantized nnUNet for Efficient and Anatomically Accurate 3D Tooth Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04201)
*Paarth Prasad,Ruchika Malhotra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拓扑约束的量化nnUNet框架，用于高效且解剖学准确的3D牙齿分割，通过引入牙科特异性拓扑损失，在保持8位量化模型计算效率的同时显著减少空间失真和拓扑错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深度学习模型量化过程中引入的空间失真问题，尤其在牙齿分割中需保持牙数、邻接关系和空腔完整性等关键解剖结构。

Method: 在量化感知训练中集成新型牙齿特异性拓扑损失；采用8位量化nnUNet主干，动态校准权重与激活；结合连通分量分析、邻接一致性与孔洞检测构建拓扑损失；联合优化交叉熵、量化正则化与拓扑约束，并用梯度近似处理持续同调项。

Result: 相比传统量化模型显著降低拓扑误差，在CBCT扫描上实现临床可接受的分割结果；支持整数-only推理，适用于资源受限的临床环境。

Conclusion: 该方法在计算效率与解剖精度之间取得良好平衡，为现实牙科应用提供了实用的医学图像分割解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a topology-constrained quantized nnUNet framework for efficient and anatomically accurate 3D tooth segmentation, addressing the challenges of spatial distortion introduced by quantization in deep learning models. The proposed method integrates a novel tooth-specific topological loss into quantization-aware training, preserving critical anatomical structures such as tooth count, adjacency relationships, and cavity integrity while maintaining computational efficiency. The system employs an 8-bit quantized nnUNet backbone, where weights and activations are dynamically calibrated to minimize precision loss during inference. Furthermore, the topological loss combines connected-component analysis, adjacency consistency, and hole detection penalties, ensuring anatomical fidelity without modifying the underlying network architecture. The joint optimization objective harmonizes cross-entropy loss, quantization regularization, and topological constraints, enabling end-to-end training with gradient approximations for persistent homology terms. Experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly reduces topological errors compared to conventional quantized models, achieving clinically plausible segmentations on dental CBCT scans. The method retains the hardware efficiency of integer-only inference, making it suitable for deployment in resource-constrained clinical environments. This work bridges the gap between computational efficiency and anatomical precision in medical image segmentation, offering a practical solution for real-world dental applications.

</details>


### [63] [Anatomy of a failure: When, how, and why deep vision fails in scientific domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04231)
*Ji-Hun Oh,Dou Hoon Kwark,Kianoush Falahkheirkhah,Kevin Yeh,John Cheville,Volodymyr Kindratenko,Rohit Bhargava*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度学习在科学成像（如红外成像）中因数据先验与模型简单性偏好不匹配而导致的严重失效问题，即使采用最先进的鲁棒化策略也难以解决，呼吁发展模态特异的安全AI算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在RGB图像上表现优异，但在科学成像（如红外成像）中效果不佳，其原因和机制尚不明确，亟需系统分析。

Method: 通过对比病理学中RGB染色图像与红外（IR）成像的深度学习性能，分析IR数据先验与DL模型简单性偏好的交互作用，并检验多种鲁棒化策略的有效性。

Result: IR数据上训练的DL模型出现一维预测坍缩，表征能力未被充分利用；现有鲁棒化方法因针对RGB设计而无法缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 通用深度学习框架在科学成像中存在固有局限，需建立面向模态特异性失效模式的研究框架，推动安全、专用AI算法的发展。

Abstract: Mirroring its ubiquity in popular media and all human activities, the use of deep learning (DL) is rapidly growing in scientific imaging modalities. However, unlike everyday RGB pictures, pixels encode precise physicochemical properties in scientific imaging across potentially thousands of channels. While DL is well validated on human-centric RGB perceptual tasks, its effectiveness for scientific imaging remains uncertain. Here, we show that the naive application of DL frameworks to scientific images can lead to critical failures. We evaluate the use of DL for pathology, comparing RGB images of stained tissue with the quantitative and information-rich biochemical signatures of infrared (IR) imaging. Despite this informational advantage, DL models trained on IR data paradoxically underperform. We investigate this discrepancy to find that IR data priors interact poorly with the simplicity bias of DL, causing models to collapse to one-dimensional predictions. This constitutes a catastrophic DL failure because the model's representational capacity remains largely unused, while furthermore raising AI safety concerns and undermining the advantages of such scientific modalities. Notably, this problem persists even with state-of-the-art DL robustification strategies, which are primarily designed and validated for RGB imagery and thus inherit the same prior-bias mismatch. This work establishes a framework for understanding the limitations of generic DL in science and advocates for the study of modality-specific failure modes to guide the development of specialized, safe AI algorithms.

</details>


### [64] [Disentangled Learning Improves Implicit Neural Representations for Medical Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04234)
*Qing Wu,Xuanyu Tian,Chenhe Du,Haonan Zhang,Xiao Wang,Le Lu,Yuyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DisINR提出一种解耦共享与个体表征的隐式神经表示框架，通过共享编解码器与个体编码器联合解码，并结合可微物理模型实现无需高质量图像的预训练和高效测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统INR对每个受试者从头训练效率低、质量差；现有初始化方法依赖高质量图像且易发生灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出DisINR框架：包含共享编码器-解码器对和个体编码器，特征联合解码重建图像；用可微分前向模型直接从有限原始测量数据预训练共享模块；测试时仅优化个体编码器，冻结共享模块。

Result: 在三个典型医学成像任务上，DisINR显著优于当前最优INR方法，在重建精度和效率两方面均有提升。

Conclusion: DisINR通过显式解耦表征和免高质量图像的预训练策略，有效提升了INR在医学成像中的泛化性、鲁棒性与实用性。

Abstract: Implicit neural representations (INRs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for medical imaging via physics-informed unsupervised learning. Classical INRs optimize an entire network from scratch for each subject, leading to inefficient training and suboptimal imaging quality. Recent initialization-based approaches attempt to inject population priors into pre-trained networks, yet they rely on high-quality images and often suffer from catastrophic forgetting during fine-tuning. We present DisINR, a novel INR framework that explicitly disentangles shared and subject-specific representations. DisINR introduces a shared encoder-decoder pair and subject-specific encoders, whose features are jointly decoded for image reconstruction. By integrating differentiable forward models, it pre-trains the shared modules directly from limited raw measurements, removing the need for pre-acquired high-quality images. During test-time adaptation, only the subject-specific encoder is optimized, while the shared pair remains frozen, effectively preserving learned priors. Extensive evaluations on three representative medical imaging tasks show that DisINR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art INRs in both reconstruction accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [65] [Densification and forecasting of Sentinel-2 time series from multimodal SAR and Optical satellite data using deep generative models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04239)
*Véronique Defonte,Dawa Derksen,Alexandre Constantin,Bastien Nespoulous*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率深度学习的框架，用于Sentinel-2光学影像时间序列的时序稠密化与未来预测，并融合Sentinel-1 SAR数据，重点建模生成图像的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云层和轨道边缘导致光学卫星影像时间序列采样不规则，现有方法仅能填补观测时段内的空缺，无法预测未来影像。

Method: 提出一种概率深度学习框架，联合利用Sentinel-2光学与Sentinel-1 SAR多模态数据，生成任意过去或未来时刻的光学影像，并显式建模生成结果的不确定性。

Result: 在稀疏且时间错位的影像序列上实现了有效的时序稠密化与未来预测。

Conclusion: 该方法突破了传统重建方法的时间范围限制，兼顾历史填补与未来预测，并通过不确定性建模提升可靠性。

Abstract: Optical satellite image time series are extensively used in many Earth observation applications, including agriculture, climate monitoring, and land surface analysis. However, clouds and swath edges result in irregular sampling along the temporal dimension, limiting continuous monitoring. To address this issue, a growing body of work has focused on temporal densification and reconstruction of satellite image time series, with the objective of filling missing or cloud-contaminated observations within the temporal extent of the available data. While these approaches improve temporal continuity, they are inherently restricted to the reconstruction of the gaps within the observed time periods, and do not address the prediction of future observations. This work proposes a probabilistic deep learning framework for the densification and forecasting of Sentinel-2 time series by generating optical images at arbitrary past or future dates. The approach leverages multimodal satellite data by jointly exploiting Sentinel-2 optical and Sentinel-1 SAR observations. Unlike most existing works, we propose to focus on the uncertainty of the generated images. Experimental results demonstrate effective densification and forecasting, on sparse and temporally misaligned time series.

</details>


### [66] [Physics-Guided Regime Unmixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04247)
*Paula Pacheco,Pablo Granitto,Juan B. Cabral*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理引导的像素级混合模型选择方法PGRU，通过可学习的注意力机制融合多种非线性模型残差，并依据物理特征动态决定每个像素应采用线性或非线性混合，提升了光谱解混精度与物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性混合模型（LMM）在存在多重散射时失效，而现有非线性模型对整幅图像统一应用固定模型，缺乏空间自适应性与物理依据。

Method: 提出Physics-Guided Regime Unmixing（PGRU），利用可观测物理特征估计每个像素的标量ξ_i∈[0,1]，以加权激活GBM、PPNM和Hapke等非线性模型的残差；通过注意力机制融合残差，生成可解释的混合‘ regime’图。

Result: 在Samson、Jasper Ridge和Urban数据集上一致优于基线方法，物理一致性指标ρ > 0.90。

Conclusion: PGRU实现了基于物理线索的自适应混合建模，兼顾解混精度与可解释性，为非线性光谱解混提供了新范式。

Abstract: The Linear Mixing Model (LMM) dominates spectral unmixing for its simplicity, but fails under multiple scattering; existing nonlinear models compensate by applying a fixed regime uniformly across entire scenes. We propose Physics-Guided Regime Unmixing (PGRU), which estimates a pixel-wise scalar $ξ_i \in [0,1]$ from observable physical features to activate nonlinear mixing only where justified. Residuals from the Generalized Bilinear Model (GBM), the Post-Nonlinear Mixing Model (PPNM), and Hapke are combined via learned attention, yielding interpretable regime maps. Experiments on Samson, Jasper Ridge, and Urban show consistent improvements over baselines, with physical coherence $ρ> 0.90$.

</details>


### [67] [Imagery Dataset for Remaining Useful Life Estimation of Synthetic Fibre Ropes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04262)
*Anju Rani,Daniel Ortiz-Arroyo,Petar Durdevic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含约34700张高分辨率图像的新数据集，涵盖11根Dyneema SK75/78绳在7种轴向载荷下的完整疲劳退化过程，每轮检测采集10张不同截面位置图像，并标注对应循环次数，用于支持RUL估计等视觉驱动的故障预测与状态监测研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏公开可用的、覆盖合成纤维绳完整退化生命周期的图像数据集，制约了数据驱动式状态监测与剩余使用寿命（RUL）估计研究。

Method: 构建基于sheave-bend测试台的受控循环疲劳实验平台，对11根HMPE绳施加60–280 kN共7级轴向载荷直至断裂；每固定周期进行一次‘检测爆发’（inspection burst），在绳不同横截面位置采集10张图像，并精确标注已运行循环数。

Result: 发布首个面向合成纤维绳全寿命退化的高分辨率图像数据集（约34,700张图像），每张图附带精确循环数标签，支持RUL回归、损伤演化建模、异常检测及载荷条件化预测等任务。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了合成纤维绳视觉化状态监测与寿命预测领域基准数据的空白，有望推动面向海洋工程、风电安装等关键场景的智能绳索健康管理技术发展。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) estimation of synthetic fibre ropes (SFRs) is critical for safe operation in offshore-crane, wind turbine installation, and heavy-load handling applications, where rope failure can result in catastrophic safety incidents and costly downtime. Despite growing research interest in data-driven condition monitoring, there is no publicly available image dataset that captures the complete degradation lifecycle of SFRs under controlled cyclic fatigue loading. To address this gap, we present a novel image dataset comprising approximately 34,700 high-resolution images of eleven Dyneema SK75/78 high-modulus polyethylene (HMPE) rope samples subjected to cyclic fatigue on a sheave-bend test stand at seven distinct axial load levels ranging from 60 kN to 280 kN. Ropes were loaded until mechanical failure, with fatigue lifetimes ranging from 695 cycles to 8,340 cycles. After every fixed number of sheave cycles (an inspection burst), ten images were captured at different cross-sectional positions along the rope, providing spatially representative sampling of surface degradation throughout the rope's entire service life. The images obtained from each load are annotated with the corresponding elapsed cycle count, enabling a direct computation of RUL for any rope in the sequence. This dataset aims to support a broad range of machine learning (ML) tasks including RUL regression, damage progression modelling, anomaly detection, and load-conditioned prognostics. The dataset is intended to serve as a benchmark resource for the development and comparison of vision-based condition monitoring (CM) and prognostics algorithms for SFRs.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond Fixed Thresholds and Domain-Specific Benchmarks for Explainable Multi-Task Classification in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04299)
*Maryam Sadat Hosseini Azad,Shahriar Baradaran Shokouhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向可解释自动驾驶的多任务视觉理解框架，通过自适应置信度阈值分析提升多任务性能，并构建了跨文化驾驶行为数据集IUST-XAI-AD，推动全球部署下可靠、可解释、文化自适应的自动驾驶系统发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在自动驾驶场景理解中缺乏透明性和安全性，亟需可解释的多任务感知系统以提升安全导航与人类信任；同时，现有评估基准缺乏针对不同驾驶文化背景的专用数据集。

Method: 提出置信度阈值敏感性分析方法，替代传统固定阈值策略，实现多任务下的自适应阈值选择；并构建新型跨文化可解释自动驾驶数据集IUST-XAI-AD（含958张图像及驾驶决策与推理的人工标注）。

Result: 自适应阈值方法显著提升各任务F1分数；IUST-XAI-AD数据集揭示了跨文化驾驶行为差异，为模型评估提供了更具挑战性的基准。

Conclusion: 本工作在方法论（自适应阈值）和实用工具（IUST-XAI-AD数据集）两方面做出贡献，有助于加速构建更可靠、可解释且文化自适应的全球自动驾驶系统。

Abstract: Scene understanding is a vital part of autonomous driving systems, which requires the use of deep learning models. Deep learning methods are intrinsically black box models, which lack transparency and safety in autonomous driving. To make these systems transparent, multi-task visual understanding has become crucial for explainable autonomous driving perception systems, where simultaneous prediction of multiple driving behaviors and their underlying explanations is essential for safe navigation and human trust in autonomous vehicles. In order to design an accurate and cross-cultural explainable autonomous driving system, we introduce a comprehensive confidence threshold sensitivity analysis that evaluates various threshold values to identify optimal decision boundaries for different tasks. Our analysis demonstrates that traditional fixed threshold approaches are suboptimal for multi-task scenarios. Through extensive evaluation, we demonstrate that our adaptive threshold selection methodology improves F1-scores across different tasks. In addition, we introduce IUST-XAI-AD, a novel dataset consisting of 958 images with human annotations for driving decisions and corresponding reasoning. This dataset addresses the critical gap in domain-specific evaluation benchmarks for distinct driving contexts and provides a more challenging test environment compared to existing datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that confidence threshold sensitivity analysis can significantly improve model performance, while the introduction of the IUST-XAI-AD dataset reveals important insights about cross-cultural driving behavior patterns. The combined contributions of this work provide both methodological advances and practical evaluation tools that can accelerate the development of more reliable, explainable, and culturally-adaptive autonomous driving systems for global deployment.

</details>


### [69] [Hierarchical Visual Agent: Managing Contexts in Joint Image-Text Space for Advanced Chart Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04304)
*Qihua Dong,Ruozhen He,Junwen Chen,Yizhou Wang,Xu Ma,Songyao Jiang,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HierVA，一种用于图表推理的分层视觉代理框架，通过在图像-文本联合空间中迭代构建和更新工作上下文，提升多子图的多步推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在理解单个图表方面表现良好，但在多个子图之间的多步推理上存在困难，而高级图表问答需要对小视觉元素的精确感知和跨多个子图的多步推理。

Method: 提出HierVA分层视觉代理框架：高层管理者生成计划并维护紧凑的关键信息上下文；专用工作者执行推理、收集证据并返回结果；分别维护视觉和文本上下文，并使用缩放工具限制视觉上下文范围。

Result: 在CharXiv推理子集上的实验表明，HierVA相比强多模态基线模型具有一致性提升；消融研究验证了分层架构、受限视觉上下文和提炼上下文各自带来互补增益。

Conclusion: HierVA通过分层设计与上下文管理机制，有效提升了多子图图表的多步推理能力，为复杂图表问答提供了新思路。

Abstract: Advanced chart question answering requires both precise perception of small visual elements and multi-step reasoning across several subplots. While existing MLLMs are strong at understanding single plots, they often struggle with multi-step reasoning across multiple subplots. We propose HierVA, a hierarchical visual agent framework for chart reasoning that iteratively constructs and updates a working context in a joint image--text space. A high-level manager generates plans and maintains a compact context containing only key information, while specialized workers perform reasoning, gather evidence, and return results. In particular, the agent maintains separate visual and textual contexts, using a zoom-in tool to restrict the visual context. Experiments on the CharXiv reasoning subset demonstrate consistent improvements over strong multimodal baselines, and ablation studies verify that hierarchical architecture, scoped visual context, and distilled context contribute complementary gains.

</details>


### [70] [InterFuserDVS: Event-Enhanced Sensor Fusion for Safe RL-Based Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04355)
*Mustafa Sakhaia,Kaung Sithua,Min Khant Soe Okea,Maciej Wielgosza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将动态视觉传感器（DVS）融入InterFuser模型的新架构，通过基于token的融合策略提升自动驾驶系统在高动态范围和高速场景下的感知鲁棒性，在CARLA基准上实现了77.2的驾驶得分和100%的路径完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RGB相机与LiDAR融合在高动态范围或高速场景中易受运动模糊和延迟影响，而事件相机（DVS）具有微秒级时间分辨率和高动态范围，有望弥补该缺陷。

Method: 扩展InterFuser模型，引入DVS作为新模态；设计基于token的融合策略，将累积事件帧嵌入Transformer主干网络，实现RGB、LiDAR与DVS三模态互补融合。

Result: 在CARLA Leaderboard基准测试中，驾驶得分为77.2，路径完成率达100%，显著提升了复杂光照与动态条件下的驾驶鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 事件相机作为补充模态可有效增强多传感器融合系统的安全性与性能，尤其适用于恶劣光照与高速动态场景。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems rely heavily on robust sensor fusion to perceive complex envi- ronments. Traditional setups using RGB cameras and LiDAR often struggle in high-dynamic- range scenes or high-speed scenarios due to motion blur and latency. Dynamic Vision Sensors (DVS), or event cameras, offer a paradigm shift by capturing asynchronous brightness changes with microsecond temporal resolution and high dynamic range. In this paper, we propose an extended architecture of the state-of-the-art InterFuser model, integrating DVS as an additional modality to enhance perception reliability. We introduce a novel token-based fusion strategy that incorporates accumulated event frames into the transformer-based backbone of InterFuser. Our method leverages the complementary nature of RGB, LiDAR, and DVS data. We evaluate our approach on the Car Learning to Act (CARLA) Leaderboard benchmarks, demonstrating that the inclusion of DVS improves the robustness of the driving agent, achieving a competitive Driving Score of 77.2 and a superior Route Completion of 100%. The results indicate that event-based vision is a promising direction for improving safety and performance in adverse lighting and dynamic conditions.

</details>


### [71] [Intermediate Representations are Strong AI-Generated Image Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04358)
*Zhenhan Huang,Pin-Yu Chen,Tejaswini Pedapati,Jianxi Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于中间层数据嵌入敏感性的搜索型方法，用于检测AI生成图像，无需训练且泛化能力强、性能优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于训练的检测方法计算成本高、泛化性差，而无训练方法性能不足，亟需兼顾高效性与准确性的新方案。

Method: 利用中间层图像嵌入对扰动的敏感性差异，通过比较原始图像与扰动图像嵌入的相似度来识别AI生成图像。

Result: 在GenImage和Forensics Small两个基准上均优于当前最优的训练式与无训练式方法；在Forensics Small上AUROC平均提升达39.61%（vs. 最佳无训练法）和5.14%（vs. 最佳训练法）。

Conclusion: 所提搜索式无训练检测方法在性能、效率与泛化性之间取得更好平衡，为AI生成图像鉴伪提供了新思路。

Abstract: The rapid advancement in generative AI models has enabled the creation of photorealistic images. At the same time, there are growing concerns about the potential misuse and dangers of generated content, as well as a pressing need for effective AI-generated image detectors. However, current training-based detection techniques are typically computationally costly and can hardly be generalized to unseen data domains, while training-free methods fall short in detection performance. To bridge this gap, we propose a search-based method employing data embedding sensitivity in intermediate layers to detect AI-generated images. Given a set of real and AI-generated images, our method examines the similarity between original image embeddings and perturbed image embeddings, and detects AI-generated images based on the similarity. We examine the proposed method on two comprehensive benchmarks: GenImage and Forensics Small. Our method exhibits improved performance across different datasets compared to both training-free and training-based state-of-the-art methods. On average, our method achieves the largest performance gain on the Forensics Small benchmark by 39.61% compared to the best training-free method and 5.14% compared to the best training-based method in AUROC score.

</details>


### [72] [Optimize-at-Capture: Highly-adaptive Exposure Controlling for In-Vehicle Non-contact Heart-rate Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04397)
*Jieying Wang,Xinqi Cai,Caifeng Shan,Wenjin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向远程光电容积描记（rPPG）的心率监测的自适应曝光控制框架，通过预测皮肤反射历史来动态调节曝光参数，显著提升了车载环境下非接触式心率测量的鲁棒性与精度，并发布了首个真实驾驶场景下的生理监测数据集ExpDrive。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有rPPG系统在智能车辆中因光照剧烈变化导致性能严重下降，关键原因在于缺乏针对rPPG优化的曝光控制——固定曝光和通用自动曝光均无法维持面部ROI亮度稳定。

Method: 提出一种高度自适应的曝光控制框架，基于历史皮肤反射建模进行前瞻性曝光参数调整；专为rPPG信号提取优化，确保皮肤ROI始终处于最佳动态范围；并构建公开数据集ExpDrive（48名被试、同步面部视频与ECG、真实驾驶场景）。

Result: 相比固定曝光和标准自动曝光，MAE从14.1 bpm降至7.79 bpm（降低6.31 bpm），成功率从24.9%提升至57.2%（+32.3个百分点，p<0.001）；在雨天（低照度）和晴天（强眩光）下均显著改善性能。

Conclusion: 曝光感知的视频采集设计对车载rPPG至关重要；所提方法有效缓解动态光照干扰，ExpDrive数据集为后续研究提供了可靠基准。

Abstract: Remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) holds great promise for continuous heart-rate monitoring of drivers in intelligent vehicles. However, its performance is severely degraded by the highly dynamic illumination changes. A critical yet overlooked factor is the lack of exposure controlling during video acquisition -- most existing systems rely on either fixed exposure settings or camera build-in auto-exposure, both of which fail to maintain stable facial brightness under rapidly changing lighting conditions during driving. To address this gap, we propose a highly-adaptive exposure controlling framework that proactively adjusts exposure parameters based on predictive modeling of historical skin reflections. Unlike standard auto-exposure, our method is specifically optimized for rPPG measurement, ensuring the skin region of interest (ROI) remains within the optimal dynamic range for rPPG signal extraction. As an important contribution of this study, we introduce ExpDrive, a public in-vehicle physiological monitoring dataset comprising synchronized facial video and reference ECG from 48 subjects captured under real driving conditions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms fixed exposure and standard auto-exposure strategies. Specifically, it reduces the Mean Absolute Error (MAE) by 6.31 bpm (from 14.1 to 7.79 bpm) and significantly increases the success rate by 32.3 percentage points (p < 0.001) (from 24.9% to 57.2%) across challenging driving scenarios. Notably, it clearly improved the performance of non-contact heart-rate monitoring in both low-light (rainy) and high-glare (sunny) conditions, validating the efficacy of exposure-aware acquisition design.

</details>


### [73] [Detecting Deepfakes via Hamiltonian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04405)
*Harry Cheng,Ming-Hui Liu,Tianyi Wang,Weili Guan,Liqiang Nie,Mohan Kankanhalli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理启发的动态稳定性分析方法HAAD，用于深伪检测，通过建模图像潜在流形为势能面并利用哈密顿动力学探测稳定性，显著提升了跨数据集泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深伪检测器依赖静态模式识别，需频繁重训练以应对新型伪造；而自然图像源于耗散物理过程，趋于稳定低能平衡态，生成模型却未显式满足几何平滑等结构约束，易产生不稳定高能状态，此物理差异可作为检测新依据。

Method: 提出哈密顿作用异常检测（HAAD）：i) 将图像潜在流形建模为势能面，真实图像对应低势能盆地响应，伪造图像引发高势能高梯度响应；ii) 引入哈密顿动力学作为稳定性探针，释放静止潜变量观察轨迹有界性；iii) 用哈密顿作用量与能量耗散两个轨迹统计量量化动态行为。

Result: HAAD在多个具挑战性的跨数据集迁移基准上显著超越当前最优方法，验证了物理启发的稳定性先验在数字取证中的有效性。

Conclusion: 将深伪检测从静态模式识别转向动态稳定性分析是可行且有效的，HAAD提供了一种不依赖特定伪造痕迹、更具泛化能力的新范式。

Abstract: Driven by the rapid development of generative AI models, deepfake detectors are compelled to undergo periodic recalibration to capture newly developed synthetic artifacts. To break this cycle, we propose a new perspective on deepfake detection: moving from static pattern recognition to dynamical stability analysis. Specifically, our approach is motivated by physics-inspired priors: we hypothesize that natural images, as products of dissipative physical processes, tend to settle near stable, low-energy equilibria. In contrast, generative models optimize for statistical similarity to real images but do not explicitly enforce structural constraints such as geometric smoothness, leaving deepfakes more likely to occupy unstable, high-energy states. To operationalize this, we introduce Hamiltonian Action Anomaly Detection (HAAD), comprising three contributions: \textbf{i)} We model the image latent manifold as a potential energy surface. Under this hypothesis, real images are expected to produce basin-like low-energy responses, whereas fake images are more likely to induce high-potential, high-gradient responses. \textbf{ii)} We employ Hamiltonian-inspired dynamics as a stability probe. By releasing latent states from rest, samples near stable regions remain bounded, while high-gradient samples produce larger trajectory responses. \textbf{iii)} We quantify these dynamic behaviors through two trajectory statistics, \ie, Hamiltonian action and energy dissipation. Extensive experiments show that HAAD outperforms evaluated state-of-the-art baselines on challenging cross-dataset transfer benchmarks, supporting a physics-inspired stability prior for digital forensics.

</details>


### [74] [UAV as Urban Construction Change Monitor: A New Benchmark and Change Captioning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04409)
*Yupeng Gao,Tianyu Li,Guoqing Wang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PTNet框架，用于遥感图像变化描述生成（RSICC），通过原型引导的任务自适应机制，联合实现变化检测与描述生成，并构建了面向城市建筑监测的UCCD新基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖隐式特征差分，缺乏对结构化变化语义的显式建模，且难以协调变化检测与描述生成在表征上的冲突；同时，当前基准对高分辨率城市建筑场景覆盖不足。

Method: 提出PTNet：1）利用可学习原型库显式建模结构化变化语义并指导跨时序交互；2）采用多头门控机制解耦任务特定表征；3）将检测获得的空间先验注入描述生成过程。同时构建UCCD数据集（9,000对图像、45,000条句子）。

Result: 在UCCD和WHU-CDC上实验表明，PTNet在变化描述与检测两方面均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PTNet通过原型引导与任务自适应设计，有效统一了变化检测与自然语言描述生成，提升了遥感图像变化理解的语义性与空间精度；UCCD数据集填补了高分辨率城市建筑变化分析的基准空白。

Abstract: Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning (RSICC) aims to generate spatially grounded natural language descriptions of scene evolution from bi-temporal imagery, moving beyond binary change masks toward semantic-level understanding. However, existing methods rely on implicit feature differencing without explicitly modeling structured change semantics, and struggle to reconcile the conflicting representation demands of change detection and caption generation. In addition, current benchmarks provide limited coverage of high-resolution urban construction scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose PTNet, a prototype-guided task-adaptive framework for joint change captioning and detection. PTNet explicitly models structured change semantics through a learnable prototype bank that guides cross-temporal interaction, disentangles task-specific representations via multi-head gating, and injects detection-derived spatial priors into caption generation, enabling coherent semantic correspondence while preserving fine-grained spatial sensitivity. Furthermore, we construct UCCD, a large-scale UAV-based benchmark comprising 9,000 high-resolution image pairs and 45,000 annotated sentences for urban construction monitoring. Extensive experiments on UCCD and WHU-CDC demonstrate that PTNet consistently outperforms existing methods. The dataset and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/G124556/ptnet.

</details>


### [75] [Evaluation Cards for XAI Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04410)
*Rokas Gipiškis,Olga Kurasova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出XAI Evaluation Card模板，旨在标准化可解释人工智能（XAI）评估方法的报告透明度，涵盖目标属性、基础层级、假设、验证证据、操纵风险与失效案例，以减少评估碎片化、支持元分析并提升研究问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI评估缺乏标准化，指标定义不一致、报告不完整、且很少与通用基线进行验证；评估报告透明度不足是核心未被充分解决的问题。

Method: 提出XAI Evaluation Card——一种类比模型卡的文档模板，用于伴随任何引入XAI评估指标的研究，覆盖目标属性、接地层级、指标假设、验证证据、游戏化风险及已知失效案例。

Result: 该模板有望减少XAI评估的碎片化，支持跨研究的元分析，并增强XAI研究的可问责性与可复现性。

Conclusion: 将XAI Evaluation Card采纳为社区规范，是提升XAI评估严谨性、一致性与可信度的关键一步。

Abstract: The evaluation of explainable AI (XAI) methods is affected by a lack of standardization. Metrics are inconsistently defined, incompletely reported, and rarely validated against common baselines. In this paper, we identify transparency of evaluation reporting as a central, under-addressed problem. We propose the XAI Evaluation Card, a documentation template analogous to model cards, designed to accompany any study that introduces an XAI evaluation metric. The card covers explicit declaration of target properties, grounding levels, metric assumptions, validation evidence, gaming risks, and known failure cases. We argue that adopting this template as a community norm would reduce evaluation fragmentation, support meta-analysis, and improve accountability in XAI research.

</details>


### [76] [Structured 3D Latents Are Surprisingly Powerful: Unleashing Generalizable Style with 2D Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04412)
*Yiran Qiao,Yiren Lu,Yunlai Zhou,Disheng Liu,Linlin Hou,Rui Yang,Yu Yin,Jing Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiLAST方法，利用预训练2D扩散模型作为教师，为3D风格迁移提供通用风格先验，通过扩散引导对齐渲染视图与目标风格，优化结构化3D潜在表示，实现对分布外（OOD）风格的鲁棒生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D风格可控生成方法在面对分布外（OOD）风格时性能显著下降或失效，亟需提升风格泛化能力。
